<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Blissey</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Ability: Natural Cure<br>Whenever you attach an Energy from your hand to this Pokémon, remove all Special Conditions from this Pokémon.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[C] Blissey Bomber: 10+ damage. This attack does 30 more damage for each Energy attached to this Pokémon. After doing damage, you may attach up to 3 Energy cards from your discard pile to this Pokémon.</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-spacer spacer-block sized vsize-1" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.spacer/37fe16c5-b343-45dd-9351-f4fe432b05d9_940/website.components.spacer.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.spacer/37fe16c5-b343-45dd-9351-f4fe432b05d9_940/website.components.spacer.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.spacer" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623679370448_1338287" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623679370448_1338287"><div class="sqs-block-content">&nbsp;</div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="ce18639e9989bfcfa9ef" id="block-ce18639e9989bfcfa9ef"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Old Cemetery</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Whenever a player attaches an Energy from their hand to one of their Pokémon (excluding [P] Pokémon), they put 2 damage counters on that Pokémon.</p>
</div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Impact Energy</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">This card can only be attached to a Single Strike Pokémon. If this card is attached to anything other than a Single Strike Pokémon, discard this card.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">As long as this card is attached to a Pokémon, it provides every type of Energy but provides only 1 Energy at a time.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">The Pokémon this card is attached to cannot be Poisoned. (If the Pokémon this card is attached to is Poisoned, it recovers from that Special Condition.)</p>
</div>
